AI BOON 
=========

In Healthcare, it is used to detect disease earlier and personalized treatment plans support.
For learning peoples, AI systems provide instant feedback on user searching questions.
Economic Efficiency: Increased productivity through automation.
Enhanced Cybersecurity: Real-time threat detection and response.
Improved Transportation: Safer and more efficient travel with autonomous vehicles.
Innovative Customer Service: 24/7 support with AI-driven chatbots and virtual assistants
disabilities people can easily access tools and applications.
Personalized content recommendations and automated content creation.
Financial Optimization: Enhanced fraud detection and personalized financial advice.
AI is not making any an error, human only making mistakes to handle.
It plays a major role for all peoples.

Bane
======
Job Displacement: Automation threatens employment.
    > In many office, Tasks performed by humans are now being executed by machines
AI collects user data, which may lead to security issues.
Sometimes user asking decision-making question that time not given processes response. 
Job Quality Reduction: Increased surveillance and control in the workplace.
Some time it provide same information again and again. 
Cybersecurity Threats: AI can be used to create more sophisticated cyberattacks.

Deforestation
====================
Causes of Deforestation
Agricultural Expansion: Clearing forests for crops and livestock.
Infrastructure Development: Land cleared from forests can be used to develop necessary infrastructure such as schools, hospitals, and other public facilities
Logging: Timber extraction for wood products and paper.
Mining: Extraction of minerals and resources.
Fires: Both natural and man-made.
Increased Revenue: Government also get more profit for the sale of timber and land
Employment Opportunities: Logging, land clearing, and subsequent agricultural and industrial activities can create jobs and reduce unemployment


Bane 
=======
Loss of Biodiversity: Deforestation leads to the destruction of habitats for many species of plants, animals, and microorganisms, resulting in a significant loss of biodiversity.
Climate Change: Trees absorb carbon dioxide. Their removal increases greenhouse gas levels in the atmosphere, contributing to global warming and climate change.
environmental damage can result in economic costs related to disaster recovery, health issues, and loss of ecosystem services.

Capturing and storing rainwater helps to conserve groundwater
It preventing soil erosion.
Rainwater harvesting and storage helps reduce flooding.
By reducing the extraction of groundwater and surface water, rainwater harvesting helps protect natural ecosystems and maintain biodiversity.
It can help to increase groundwater levels
Provides a reliable source of water during periods of drought or water restrictions, ensuring water availability even in dry seasons.
Rainwater is relatively free of pollutants and salts, making it suitable for various uses and reducing the need for extensive water treatment.

Is Technology making us less human?
======================================
PROS:
Technology allows us to stay connected with people across the globe, fostering relationships
Apps and online platforms provide access to mental health resources and support.
Technology provides unprecedented access to information, facilitating learning and intellectual growth.
Technological advancements in healthcare, such as telemedicine, wearable health monitors, and advanced diagnostic tools, improve the quality of care and accessibility for patients.
Money transfer also very fast with ATM and mobile banking also.


CONS:
Excessive use of technology, particularly social media, can lead to feelings of isolation and loneliness.
The instant gratification provided by technology can shorten attention spans and reduce the ability to engage in deep, focused thinking.
Loss of Privacy: Extensive use of technology often comes with privacy sacrifices, as personal information can be easily collected, stored, and misused.



Importance for kindness
===========================
Kindness is the quality of being friendly, generous, considerate, and empathetic towards others. 
I believe kindness can really boost our own happiness and mental health. When we are kind to others, it makes us feel good about ourselves.
Kindness fosters positive relationships. It builds trust, strengthens connections, and creates a supportive
 If we believe someone needs our support, we stand by them
A culture of kindness can transform communities. It promotes cooperation, reduces conflict, and creates a sense of belonging and mutual respect
Kindness in the workplace can lead to increased job satisfaction, productivity, and employee retention. It creates a positive work culture where individuals feel valued and supported.
Being kind can reduce stress, worry, and depression. It can also have physical health benefits, such as lowering blood pressure and improving heart health.
Kindness can be especially important during times of crisis or adversity. It can provide much-needed emotional support

Conclusion 
==============
kindness can create significant positive changes in our lives and the lives of others.


Kindness is all that our world needs today in the era of excessive competition











Day 4 Homework
=================
am + verb + ing
================
I am reading a novel.
I am cooking dinner for my family.
I am studying for my exams.
I am enjoying the beautiful weather.
I am writing a letter to my father.

is + verb + ing
================
He is reading a novel.
She is cooking dinner for my family.
He is studying for his exams.
She is enjoying the beautiful weather.
She is writing a letter to her father.

are + verb + ing
================
They are reading a novel.
We are cooking dinner for my family.
We are studying for our exams.
They are enjoying the beautiful weather.
They are writing a letter to their father.

is/are/am => not + Noun
==========================
I am not reading a novel.
She is not cooking well.
He is not studying for his exams.
We are not cooking dinner for my family.
They are not enjoying the beautiful weather.

Day 5 Homework
=================
was + Noun
================
Yesterday was a challenge.
The meeting was a success.
Her performance was a masterpiece.
That movie was a disappointment.
His reaction was a surprise.

were + Noun
================
The toys were new.
The flowers were beautiful.
The books were interesting.
The cookies were delicious.
The movies were fun.

was / were + not + Noun
=======================
The toys were not new.
The flowers were not beautiful.
The books were not interesting.
That movie was not a disappointment.
The meeting was not a success.


Day 6 Homework
=================
was + verb + ing
================
I was reading a novel.
She was cooking dinner for my family.
He was studying for my exams.
She was enjoying the beautiful weather.
She was writing a letter to her father.

were + verb + ing
===================
They were reading a novel.
We were cooking dinner for my family.
We were studying for our exams.
They were enjoying the beautiful weather.
They were writing a letter to their father.

is/are/am => not + Noun
==========================
I was not reading a novel.
She was not cooking well.
He was not studying for his exams.
We were not cooking dinner for my family.
They were not enjoying the beautiful weather.


Day 7 Homework
=================
will be + Noun
==========================
The homework will be done tomorrow.
Our vacation will be in July.
His presentation will be tomorrow.
The meeting will be at noon.
The new restaurant will be opening soon.

will not be + Noun
==========================
The homework will not be done tomorrow.
Our vacation will not be in July.
His presentation will not be tomorrow.
The meeting will not be at noon.
The new restaurant will not be opening soon.


Day 8 Homework
=================
will be + verb + ing
==========================
I will be studying all weekend for my exams.
She will be traveling to Europe next month.
The children will be playing in the park this afternoon.
The company will be launching a new product tomorrow.
They will be renovating their house.

will not be + verb + ing
==========================
I will not be studying all weekend for my exams.
She will not be traveling to Europe next month.
The children will not be playing in the park this afternoon.
The company will not be launching a new product tomorrow.
They will not be renovating their house.

In my opinion, if the same client provides different feedback, they should use the same ticket. 


Regarding the mercerinsigniaTESTONLY theme ticket, I'm facing an issue, Dan. The BA attached some feedback to the ticket, and after three days, I picked up the ticket and fixed the mentioned feedback. However, in the meantime, the BA changed some template structures and made updates to the CSS file as well. These changes were not reflected in my ticket files. When I was about to push my changes, I realized that around 100+ lines of CSS had been altered in over 20 instances.

It's too complicated to manage without the latest information. In my opinion, since the BA is aware of the changes made to the templates or CSS files, it would be helpful if they attached the latest PDFs. This way, we can work with the most current structure and CSS files from the start. Having to work with outdated files and then request the new ones to fix all the changes not only takes more time but also leads to potential confusion.

React interview questions 
================================

api calling - axios or any other methods

functional components creation, redux and react query
react typescript knowledge
3rd party ui libraries experiences eg MUI 
architecture level understanding

Scenario: Verification Code Screen layout and content - Resend OTP flow
Given the user is on the "Verification Code" screen
Then the screen displays the following content:
| Enter Code (input) |
| Submit (primary action button) |
| Resend Code (clickable link) |
| Back  (secondary action button) |


Scenario: Error handling on Verification Code Screen
Given: the user enters an incorrect or expired verification code
Then: the system displays the error message:
"""
The verification code you entered is incorrect or has expired. Please double-check and try again.
"""

Scenario: The user did not get the OPT email
When: the user click on the "Resend OTP"
Then: another the system will send the user the Verification code to their registered email address

Scenario: Navigating to Pin Reset Successful page

Given the user has entered a valid verification code
When the user clicks "Verify Code"

Then the user is redirected to the "Pin Reset Successful page" screen
And an email is sent to the email linked to the student number with the reset Pin.







import {inject} from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn,Router  } from '@angular/router';
import {AccountService } from '../_services';
export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = () => {
  const router = inject(Router);
  const accountService = inject(AccountService);
  const account = accountService.accountValue;   
  if (account && account.token) {     
    return true;
  } else {
    router.navigate(['/auth'],{ replaceUrl: true });
    return false;
  }
};


  logout() {  
    sessionStorage.removeItem('SessionAccount');  
    this.router.navigate(['/auth'], { replaceUrl: true }).then(() => {
      history.replaceState(null, '', location.href);
      location.reload(); 
    });
  }
  
  
  
 import { NgModule } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterModule, Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { authGuard } from './_helpers';

const routes: Routes = [
  {
    path: 'auth',
    loadChildren: () => import('./auth/auth.module').then((m) => m.AuthModule),
  },
  {
    path: 'app',    
    loadChildren: () => import('./main/main.module').then((m) => m.MainModule),
    canActivate:[authGuard],
  },
  { path: '', redirectTo: '/auth', pathMatch: 'full' }  // Default route to auth
];

@NgModule({
  imports: [RouterModule.forRoot(routes)],
  exports: [RouterModule],
})
export class AppRoutingModule {}
